How Much Are Delta SkyMiles Worth Compared To American AAdvantage Miles In 2026?

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

MC MEDIASTUDIO & Karolis Kavolelis | Shutterstock, Simple Flying

It can be difficult for airlines to truly distinguish themselves because all carriers fundamentally sell the same product: flights. Therefore, airlines need to find ways to differentiate the experience of flying from point A to point B, and that's where loyalty programs come in. By rewarding high-value customers with perks and benefits, airlines can essentially keep customers tied to their network and larger frequent flyer ecosystem.

This is an incredibly lucrative side of the business. Within the US, American Airlines' AAdvantage program was previously the US's largest airline loyalty program, but it has been overtaken by Delta Air Lines' SkyMiles. The two programs, while being similar in many ways, also have a variety of differences.

These may make one or the other more appealing to a specific customer. What's more, the individual value of a mile issued by these two programs is not necessarily the same. It's therefore important to know what the value proposition for each program is and which one is best suited for you.

The true value of airline miles is not a set figure, because this can change based on individual redemptions. However, it is possible to estimate mile valuations at a specific point in time based on systemwide redemption prices. NerdWallet estimates the value of one AAdvantage mile at 1.7 cents, while estimating that one SkyMile is worth 1.2 cents.

Meanwhile, The Points Guy estimates that one AAdvantage mile is worth 1.45 cents, while pegging the value of one SkyMile at 1.2 cents. Across the board, AAdvantage miles are considered to be more valuable than SkyMiles, and this is a unique advantage for American. NerdWallet pegs a United Airlines MileagePlus mile at 1.2 cents, while The Points Guy values MileagePlus miles the same.

There has been criticism of Delta and United for the devaluation of their programs' miles, and AAdvantage offers a material advantage in terms of redemption values. This is important when trying to decide which airline to be loyal to. At all three airlines, you can earn miles by flying or through credit card spending, which has become the primary means of mileage earnings in the US.

The airlines offer a wide range of credit cards, each coming with progressively higher annual fees and increasingly lucrative benefits. In addition, all three carriers are part of airline alliances, which grant certain benefits when traveling with select airlines worldwide, and they also have individual partnerships with airlines outside of the alliances.

Delta Air Lines uses four main Medallion tiers, as well as the general membership status and the invite-only Delta 360 tier at the very top. The four Medallion tiers, named Silver, Gold, Platinum, and Diamond, offer increasing rewards with each higher tier. In order, they respectively require 5,000, 10,000, 15,000, and 28,000 Medallion Qualifying Dollars.

These can be earned through flying or through credit card spending, with Delta not requiring members to actually fly for Medallion status. Similarly, American Airlines has its general membership tier, its invite-only ConciergeKey status, and four main elite status tiers: Gold, Platinum, Platinum Pro, and Executive Platinum. It uses Loyalty Points as the metric for status, requiring 40,000, 75,000, 125,000, and 200,000 Loyalty Points for each tier.

Crucially, however, it's much easier to earn Loyalty Points with American than MQDs with Delta, as the airline essentially awards one Loyalty Point for every eligible base mile earned. Delta awards one MQD for every dollar spent on the base ticket price and associated airline fees on Delta-operated flights, as well as bookings with Delta Vacations.

In addition, flights operated by close Delta partners earn MQDs based on a calculation of fare and distance, while SkyMiles co-branded credit cards use their own calculation: the SkyMiles Platinum and Business Platinum cards earn $1 MQD per $20 spent, while the Reserve and Business Reserve seats earn $1 MQD for every $10 spent.

SkyMiles and AAdvantage both offer four personal credit cards, with American Express issuing the SkyMiles co-branded credit cards while Citi issues the AAdvantage co-branded cards. The top-end AAdvantage Executive World Legend Mastercard notably provides unlimited Admirals Club access, whereas the top-end Delta SkyMiles Reserve Amex only grants 15 Sky Club visits annually unless the cardholder reaches $75,000 in annual spend.

This is a significant difference. As previously mentioned, the AAdvantage cards provide a quicker path to earning elite status. Both sets of cards offer a variety of benefits, and the choice of which one to choose is largely down to personal preference. However, the benefits of going with SkyMiles are that miles never expire, whereas AAdvantage miles expire after 24 months of account activity.

In addition, AAdvantage only offers one business card, whereas SkyMiles offers three, making the latter a better choice for this demographic. The AAdvantage Platinum Select World Elite Mastercard has a lower annual fee than the comparable SkyMiles Gold Amex ($99 versus $150).

Meanwhile, the AAdvantage Executive World Legend Mastercard has a higher annual fee than the SkyMiles Reserve Amex ($695 versus $650). The two airlines offer sign-on bonuses, usually in the form of bonus miles (although they can include more). Both sets of cards also grant status boosts in the form of extra Loyalty Points/MQDs in some cases, although it's still easier to earn AAdvantage Loyalty Points.

American Airlines offers its members multiple additional perks for reaching a certain amount of Loyalty Points. This is solely tied to the number of Loyalty Points reached in a calendar year, with the lowest milestone being 15,000 Loyalty Points. There are also multiple checkpoints, each offering a small variety of benefits that are different from the other milestones.

As a whole, the benefits list is quite diverse, allowing AAdvantage members across the board to receive multiple perks throughout the year. Delta Air Lines also offers SkyMiles members additional perks under the 'Choice Rewards' system. However, these are only available to Platinum and Diamond members.

Still, the rewards list is extensive for both tiers, with Platinum Medallion members able to choose options such as regional upgrade certificates, an MQD accelerator, bonus miles, a charitable donation, a status gift, statement credit, Starbucks Rewards credit, Delta Vacations credit, a Sustainable Aviation Fuel credit, or a Wheels Up credit. Diamond members get even more choices.

Delta is a SkyTeam member, and the SkyMiles program provides reciprocal SkyTeam status for the appropriate tier: SkyTeam Elite for Silver Medallion, and SkyTeam Elite Plus for the higher tiers. American Airlines, on the other hand, is a oneworld member, and oneworld offers three status tiers for loyalty program members.

The Emerald tier slots above anything that SkyTeam or Star Alliance offers in terms of benefits. This is a major incentive for AAdvantage members to chase the program's top two tiers, which grant oneworld Emerald status. SkyMiles does not publish an awards chart, as all award flights are priced dynamically, and American AAdvantage also does this for the most part.

However, AAdvantage does still use an awards chart for partner-operated flights, publishing estimated starting rates by region. What's more, American allows for upgrades from economy straight to business class (excluding basic economy fares), whereas Delta generally only permits upgrades to the next cabin class.

Multiple Delta Air Lines credit cards will help you earn elite status.

For some, there are benefits that make SkyMiles a lucrative program, such as miles that never expire, or the wider range of business credit cards. On the surface, however, there are several benefits to going for AAdvantage over SkyMiles, such as the greater upgrade flexibility, oneworld Emerald status, Admirals Club access, the Loyalty Points system, and the generally superior value per mile.

Still, two important considerations remain, which might triumph over everything else. The first is that passengers generally consider Delta to offer a better passenger experience. Although the Sky Clubs are harder to access, they're generally considered to be nicer, while nearly all Delta narrowbodies offer seatback entertainment.

SkyMiles members aren't necessarily choosing the program over competitors because of the merits of the program, but rather because they want to travel with Delta, and this may be more important to some than certain benefits offered by AAdvantage. However, arguably the most important consideration is location.

Regardless of which program's perks are more valuable, this matters little if someone lives in Atlanta, a major Delta hub, or Dallas/Fort Worth, a major American hub. The network and schedules that the dominant airline in a fortress hub offers usually have a much greater effect on which loyalty program to join. Some airports are more competitive, including smaller spokes as well as hubs like New York or Los Angeles, but even then, network and schedule play a major role.

Here's the Average Social Security Benefit by Age 62 to 80

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Claiming at 62 versus 70 creates a gap that reshapes your entire retirement income picture.

Social Security provides a vital floor for your retirement, but it's meant to supplement your income rather than replace it entirely. The amount you receive depends heavily on when you choose to claim, making it one of the most consequential financial decisions you'll face.

The Social Security Administration (SSA) calculates your benefit based on an average of your wages across your 35 highest-earning years, adjusted for inflation and run through a specific formula. Your final check size is then determined by your full retirement age (FRA) and the age you begin collecting.

Here is the average monthly retired-worker benefit by age, as of December 2025, the most recent data available.

The data reveals a clear increase between ages 62 and 70, with the average benefit growing by 59.7%. This represents a monthly gap of $850.28, or $10,203.36 per year.

Full retirement age (FRA) ranges from 66 to 67, depending on your birth year, and it's 67 for everyone born in 1960 or later. If you claim at 62, you face a permanent reduction of up to 30%, while waiting until 70 earns you an 8% annual increase through delayed retirement credits.

A second pattern appears after age 70, as averages begin to decline slightly. This doesn't mean the SSA cuts benefits for individuals. Instead, older groups likely include more people who claimed early at permanent reductions.

Women also make up a larger share of the oldest groups because they tend to live longer. Because they often have lower lifetime earnings due to the gender pay gap and time spent caregiving, their presence in the data lowers the overall average among the oldest retirees.

Even at the age-70 peak of $2,274.68 per month, or $27,296.16 per year, benefits rarely cover every bill. That peak falls $34,135.84 short of the $61,432 average senior household spending reported by the Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS).

A 3.6% cost-of-living adjustment (COLA) will take effect in 2027, adding about $82 a month on top of that age-70 benefit. While these adjustments are designed to keep pace with inflation, they often fall short.

The most useful number for your planning is the average that matches your own intended claiming age.

Even those who claim Social Security benefits at age 70, when they qualify for their maximum checks, rarely live comfortably on their benefits alone. You'll probably need other sources of retirement income to cover what Social Security doesn't.

Personal savings are your best bet if they're available to you. You're free to spend that money on anything you like, but remember it may have to last you 20 years or more, so choose a withdrawal strategy designed to make it last. If you don't have a lot of personal savings, you may need a job or other government benefits to supplement your Social Security checks.

Make sure you come up with a plan for how you'll cover your remaining living expenses before you apply for Social Security. If you're married, be sure to factor in your spouse's Social Security checks and personal savings as well, and make sure you're both on the same page about when each person plans to claim.

For more on how Social Security affects your retirement, see this guide.

Trump offers UFC chief Dana White press secretary job

📅 2026-11-07  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

President Donald Trump offered UFC president and CEO Dana White the job of press secretary on Saturday.

In a post on Truth Social, the president said the offer extended to White's assistants, "even though some are not from our Country."

"We need these people to defend us against the Lunatic Left," Trump wrote. "Dana will be a Press Secretary like none other. Dana, please take the job!"

Apparently in response to Trump's post, White posted a picture of himself on Instagram stories standing behind the press secretary's lectern in the White House, with the caption, "On to the next chapter." It was unclear whether White has accepted the position or is simply acknowledging the president's request in jest.

In October, White House press secretary Katie Zacharia left the press secretary position, just five days after her stint as Karole Levitt's replacement began.

The president told reporters he suggested Zacharia quit after her first and only press briefing, where in her six minutes before White House reporters she only took questions from three.

"I just spoke to her about it. I said, 'Katie, is this for you?'" Trump said outside the White House on Oct. 16.

The president's appeal to White comes after Friday's announcement that Trump's deputy chief of staff, Stephen Miller, would be promoted to chief of staff, replacing Susie Wiles.

Trump revealed the stunning shake-up just three days after Democrats won a landslide victory in the midterm elections, gaining majority control in the House and Senate and scrambling the trajectory of Trump's last two years in office.

The president and White have a longstanding friendship. During Trump's first campaign in 2016, White praised the then-Republican nominee and called him a "fighter."

"Nobody took us seriously. Nobody. Except Donald Trump. Donald was the first guy to recognize the potential we saw in the UFC and encouraged us to build our business," White said.

And for his 80th birthday in June, President Trump hosted a UFC match — dubbed UFC Freedom 250 — on the White House's South Lawn.

The White House and the UFC didn't immediately respond to a request for comment.

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Now it becomes real for UCLA.The Bruins were No. 8 in the first round of College Football Playoff rankings, which doesn't matter. If they end up 10-2, as a Big Ten team, they're a mortal lock to get into the CFP. But at this point, they want a spot in the Big Ten Championship, and they're good enough to beat anyone left on the slate.Minnesota has proven it's almost good enough to beat anyone, losing a brutal 23-16 heartbreaker to Indiana last week. It beat Iowa and Michigan, but a weird 41-13 loss at Purdue is an issue for an at-large CFP spot. However, win out, and there's a shot at getting into the Big Ten title game.

UCLA gets Illinois in the Rose Bowl next, then has to travel to Michigan, and finishes up down the road at USC.

Minnesota goes to Penn State next week, hosts Northwestern, and close with the Paul Bunyan Axe game at Wisconsin.

The two programs have only faced each other four times, with Minnesota winning the last meeting 21-17 in 2024. The Gophers are 3-1, starting with a 21-3 win in the 19162 Rose Bowl.

Date: Saturday, November 7, 2026Game Time: 12:00 PM ETTV: FOXLocation: Huntington Bank Stadium, Minneapolis, MN– Week 10 Schedule, Predictions– CFN Top 25 | Bowl Projections– Expert Picks: Week 10

Run, run, and keep running.There's no real mystery to what UCLA does. It blasts away, with the ground game, gets just enough from the passing game to get by, and then it relies on the defense to take care of everything else. It starts with third down stops. Only Nebraska – at least before the loss to Illinois – is better at defensively on third downs than the Bruins. That allows UCLA to dominate the time of possession battle, the rested defense forces a ton of takeaways, the offense capitalizes … repeat. However …

UCLA runs well, Minnesota doesn't. Minnesota can throw well, UCLA doesn't. Other than that, these two do almost all of the same things.You want third down defense? Minnesota is sixth in the nation, allowing teams to convert just 28% of their chances.Time of possession? The Gophers aren't as good as UCLA, but they're fine, usually keeping the ball for close to 32 minutes per game. And then there are the takeaways – Minnesota is good at that, too.

The whole Big Noon Kickoff aspect to this is a problem for UCLA.The Bruins have to be ready to rumble at 9:00 am Los Angeles time. And then there's the other issue. When the ball gets kicked off in Minneapolis, it will be around 71 degrees in LA.The temperature should hover around 40 degrees and feel more like 32ish for the entire game in Huntington Bank.UCLA will be pushed and tested, and Minnesota will take advantage of that early, but the score will be kept low throughout, and the Bruins will still be alive in the second half, when the ground game starts to take over.It'll be another tremendously entertaining Minnesota game to kick off a whale of a Saturday.

Huddersfield Town must demand £7m-£8m for 'unplayable' star better than Sorba Thomas

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Huddersfield Town are flying at the top of League One right now, and if the Terriers can maintain this level of performance and form throughout the course of the campaign, the Championship beckons, though one player will be key to those efforts.

Huddersfield sit at the top of League One right now with eight wins, five draws, and just one defeat in 14 league games thus far, as Martin Drury has his side playing some excellent stuff.

The likes of Archie Collins, Ibane Bowat, and Ilias Bronkhorst headed to the ACCU Stadium over the summer, and following Bronkhorst from Excelsior to West Yorkshire was Dutch winger Derensili Sanches Fernandes, who has captured the attention of the entire EFL with his scintillating performances so far.

We rank all 24 League One stadiums by their ground's distance from the city or town, starting with the longest distance.

Sanches Fernandes, 25, has quickly become Huddersfield's star man up top, as alongside Welsh forward Joe Taylor, the winger is absolutely thriving right now, with seven goals and six assists in just 14 league games so far.

Sanches Fernandes trails only MK Dons' Aaron Collins in the combined goals and assists numbers in League One right now, but it would be no surprise to see the 25-year-old overtake the former Bolton Wanderers striker before too long.

The Dutchman, of Cape Verdean descent, has already played down exit talk following his excellent performances, telling Dutch media outlet Voetbal: "It's all happening way too fast. I also read those crazy things, for example, that I'll be playing in the Premier League as early as winter.

"I'm not thinking about that at all right now; whatever happens, happens, but I'm going to fight to bring Huddersfield back to the level where they belong. His dream of playing in the Premier League himself is very much alive. I am still two steps away."

Football League World's Huddersfield fan pundit, Graeme Rayner, has shared their thoughts on what Sanches Fernandes' asking fee should be right now amid an incredible start to life in West Yorkshire.

Graeme said: "Derensili Sanches Fernandes, until the summer, I would wager that pretty much no Huddersfield Town fan, except perhaps the Football Manager computer game geeks, had heard of him, and even those might not have.

"He came out of nowhere in terms of a purchase for us, and the scouting that's been involved has clearly been fantastic, because both he and Ilias Bronkhorst link really well on the right-hand side, and he has been the standout player in League One this season.

"He's been phenomenal. I think he's scored seven and got six assists, I believe it is. So he's got a goal involvement pretty much every game. That's 13 goal involvements in 14 League One games.

"He is quite often pivotal in build-up play to goals where he doesn't get the assist as well. His use of the bullish phenomenon, his ability to run at players is great; his touch is great, he's strong, he's quick. At times, he has looked unplayable at League One level. And having watched a lot of Championship football over recent years, I would say that he'd probably be very effective in the Championship as well.

"In terms of his price tag, the good news is Huddersfield Town don't need to sell, and he's already said that he wants to stay. So the good news is that in January, if someone wants to come in, they're going to have to pay a lot of money. I would say his market value, therefore, is probably somewhat inflated.

"Sorba Thomas was the last right-winger we had at the club who was involved in as many goals as he is. In the summer, I believe he went for around £7 million, with potential add-ons, for a £10 million transfer to Hull City. A lot of Town fans already, and we have to bear in mind he's playing League One, think he's better than Sorba Thomas and his ceiling might be higher.

"But I would say if anybody comes in with an offer of less than £6 million-£7 million in January, it wouldn't even be considered. Obviously, every player has their price. If someone comes in with £7 million-£8 million, maybe we'd accept it. I don't know. But I would say it's going to be silly money. It's got to be silly money because it's more important to us.

"The value to us is higher by getting promoted than selling him. So that's what I would say. I just don't think that we need to sell him."

What have you made of Sanches Fernandes so far? What would you value him at? Does he have a higher ceiling than Sorba Thomas? Share your thoughts below!

Naturally, if a player is being linked with a move from League One to the Premier League, it takes great character to cancel that outside noise and remain focused purely on the current situation.

As for Sanches Fernandes, he appears to be doing just that, and if he does indeed commit the entire campaign to Huddersfield and remains fit, the Terriers are surely a Championship club in waiting.

Under contract until the summer of 2030 and showing no signs of forcing an exit, Sanches Fernandes would command a lucrative fee for Huddersfield, and no matter how much their resolve is tested, it will surely take a huge number to prise him away from West Yorkshire anytime soon.

Infantino caught in the web as tide turns against Trump in midterms and leaves Fifa exposed | Barney Ronay

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Trump Exposed Reactor Core Syndrome very much in play after Democrat landslide – and Fifa’s president may pay a price

Oh baby, clamp me in your repulsive mouthparts. Sink your unusually large and venomous fangs into my abdomen. And yes, we are doing this in the White House cabinet rooms, out there in plain sight of the watching world, just a gleaming golden ball between us. But it’s fine. Probably. Now take a sip of those sweet, sweet tiny male arachnoid entrails.

An exciting new study was published over the summer into the habits of the Australian redback spider. The research made headlines for its focus on reproductive behaviour, specifically the male’s urgent and very willing engagement in what is known as sexual cannibalism, AKA copulatory suicide.

Scientists observing these tiny arachnoids discovered that during the act of lovemaking with the larger, more decorative female, the male will perform a spectacular sexual death-somersault, flipping himself upside down, presenting his underbelly to the female’s fangs, and urging her to literally consume his intestines mid-coitus as an energy snack and self-dooming sweetener. You’ve already ripped my heart out, babe. Now take my guts as well.

This doesn’t paint a very flattering picture of the male redback’s sense of himself, his ability to recognise boundaries in a relationship. Overall, the picture is of a craven, fawning, hairless entity, entirely in thrall to its power-master. Yes, it might make sense for a while to place your prospects in the hands of a glassy-eyed red-tinged predator at the heart of a toxic web of power. But it can land you in a spot of bother down the line.

In unrelated news, there was a sense this week that Gianni Infantino’s entanglements with Donald Trump may have now moved one step closer to scrutiny, sunlight, consequences, perhaps even some kind of decisive intestinal devouring. The sight of Infantino cavorting for the US president was one of the more grotesque sport-adjacent spectacles of recent times. What kind of relationship was this?

How will history remember that display of power-fluffing, the supposedly apolitical Fifa president sat nodding gravely at the walnut-veneered table as the latest immigration policy is trailed, beaming along as some random crypto-waffle is chucked into the mix, and becoming through this an ever more contorted figure, as though essence of human mendacity has been condensed, reconstituted into a fine paste, pumped into a serge blue suit and taught to walk around saying the phrase: “My magic ball unites the golden peace world.”

At the time it felt as though this was some kind of warning siren, a real-time satire on the idea of unaccountable executive power. Is this just allowed now, confirmation in human form that acting as though you exist outside the rules is halfway to actually being there?

Well, maybe not. As of Wednesday there is for the first time a sense of something potentially self-dooming about that public two-hander. Perhaps this is the best way to picture those interactions now, Infantino backflipped in supplication, thighs clamped around the presidential shoulders, presenting his tenderest parts to those razor-sharp mandibles. Feast on my entrails. Consume my borrowed sporting soul. Empower me. Or don’t. Whatever. We’ll talk later.

Trump may be heading into a diminished final two years in office. But Trump Exposed Reactor Core Syndrome is still very much in play, a principle that states, yes, he will give you heat, light and a deathly glow. But he will also melt your face in the process. And maybe this process is now finally coming for the world’s second-most-provocative elected president.

The headline Fifa news this week was the council member Kozo Tashima’s announcement that he will stand as a candidate in March’s leadership vote. There is, at the very least, someone else to vote for now. In the wider world, but potentially more significant to the Infantino regime, the US midterm elections brought a Democratic landslide in the Senate and the House of Representatives.

Football is, as ever, just a tiny notch on the to-do list. To date those results are simply a rejection of something, the chance to luxuriate in the defenestrating of some of those High Trump oddities, the senator who believes a cabal of Sikhs is trying to steal the moon, the House representative on record as saying all Eritreans are genetically derived from yoghurt, the one-term governor who looks as if he bites the heads off sparrows and wears a suit made entirely from recycled human fingernails.

What comes next is not clear. But there is talk of cleaning house. Internal judicial matters are now firmly in the hands of Trump’s opponents. Some Democrats have warned of the need to “lawyer up”, have spoken about correcting the most excessive acts of opportunism over the past two years.

Even as Infantino is out there bartering for his future, bothered at mosquito-level by a cloud of revelations – the use of Fifa resources to electioneer, talk of having his maid’s visa raised at government level – the one thing that may actually get close to him is force majeure.

At which point enter the tantalising figure of Jamie Raskin, representative for Maryland’s 8th district, and widely tipped to become the new chair of the House judiciary committee. Raskin is a hawk on what he describes as “the corruption” of aspects of the Trump regime. And these include Fifa.

Kick off your evenings with the Guardian's take on the world of football

Raskin’s letter to Infantino dated 26 July bears re-reading. First he brings up the wire fraud and money laundering case against Full Play Group that was part of the 2015 indictments against the Blatter regime and that was dropped by the Trump administration because it was “not a priority”.

He refers to “Fifa’s brazen campaign to curry favour with Donald Trump since his re-election”, the “hilariously fake ‘Fifa Peace Prize’”, the leasing of office space in Trump Tower at an economically irrational rate, describing this and other elements as a “strategy of outright bribery”.

Examples of price gouging and “fraudulent sales tactics” are mentioned as a lever for a potentially fascinating disclosure of Fifa communications with the Trump organisation, and the promise of a full congressional investigation.

This was sabre-rattling at the time. It isn’t any more. The letter was also sent before the Joshua Kushner-linked scheme to sell off part of the World Cup to private equity, described by Raskin as a “smash-and-grab scam of colossal proportions”. “Now we see where all the bribes, kickbacks and sweetheart deals have been leading,” he told the Guardian. Are you excited yet? Does this sound like possible fangs of doom stuff?

This is the same Democrat who could now have the authority to compel testimony and documents from Fifa, JP Morgan – the bank involved in the sell-off process – and entities associated with the Kushner family. The Trump White House has in the past dismissed such allegations as politically motivated. Fifa has kept its head down. Well, good luck with that now.

Who knows? Even the noise, the heat and the potential ripples may be enough for some of those being asked to vote in Rabat next year. Infantino’s self-promotion as a one-man global brand, the royal court persona, the selfie with Pelé’s coffin, the micromanaging of the vote for Saudi 2034. All this creates its own internal tensions.

There would of course be satisfaction at witnessing a redback-style ending. Even if, as ever, the worst part is the feeling of sadness, of chances missed, the thought of what could have been done with all this power in larger, less grasping, less mandible-like hands.

The wheels of justice can be disappointingly clogged and reluctant to grind. But this feels motivated and public enough to carry some energy. There is at least a ball rolling now. Plus the central irony, that Infantino made himself a player here, dared the world to watch, and willingly hurled himself into that cannibalistic embrace.

From Rihanna to Rosalía, These Are Martine Rose’s Biggest Celebrity Superfans

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

At last, the news has officially broken: Martine Rose has been appointed to the helm of Balenciaga. While expectations of the announcement have been brewing for the past few months, it’s one that has been met with pretty much ubiquitous celebration.

That’s not to dismiss the achievements of her predecessor, Pierpaolo Piccioli, whose tenure was marked by a sculptural, romantic flair and a rich sense for color and textural play. Still, the warmth expressed for the British designer’s appointment is uniquely multifaceted. There’s its representational significance, as she joins a growing coterie of women of color at the helm of fashion’s most influential houses, but beyond that—and arguably more importantly—is her skill as a designer, and its capacity to shape the fashion conversation at large.

That’s something she’s more than proved at her namesake label, where her tweaked-and-twisted, often quietly kinky riffs on the everyday dress codes she observes in her native London have been key in bridging the gap between the realms of streetwear and high fashion over the past decade. There is also, as anyone versed in their menswear lore will know, the fact that Rose’s big break arguably came at Balenciaga, where she played an instrumental role in shaping the aesthetic of the house, working alongside Demna during the early, agenda-setting years of his tenure. And then, as we’ve come to know over the years, there’s Martine Rose herself: one of the sharpest and funniest voices the industry has in its midst.

You don’t have to look far for proof of just how widely her influence has spread. Though often framed as something of a fashion insider’s favorite, Rose has an equally devoted A-list following. Rihanna is regularly spotted in her designs, while Kendrick Lamar has all but appointed her his de facto dresser for some of the biggest moments of his career, wearing custom Rose looks to collect his Best Rap Album Grammy in 2023 and for his critically acclaimed 2025 Super Bowl half-time performance.

Below, revisit the moments your favorite celebrities repped one of fashion’s favorite designers.

In October 2025, Justin Bieber was spotted in LA wearing a graphic, color-blocked, logo-emblazoned sweater by Martine Rose.

Rihanna is a Martine Rose superfan. In May 2025, she was seen sporting one of the designer’s signature striped polos and baggy-fit jeans on the streets of New York, shortly after revealing her baby bump at that year’s Met Gala.

We’re naturally biased, but one of our favorite Martine Rose moments is without doubt when Billie Eilish graced the cover of our British Vogue’s May 2025 issue, sporting a hot-pink track jacket and headscarf in an updated riff on Balmoral chic.

At the 2025 Super Bowl, Kendrick Lamar—another of Martine Rose’s highest profile superfans—wore a full custom look by the designer for his critically-lauded half-time show, single-handedly reinvigorating the market for denim flares.

In 2025, Timothée Chalamet opted for Martine Rose tailoring for the London premiere of A Complete Unknown, shortly after being spotted in LA wearing one of the brand’s distressed caps.

Kendrick Lamar’s penchant for wearing the brand goes back further than his Super Bowl performance. In 2023, he wore a full look—including a customized track jacket and a then-unreleased colorway of the designer’s viral Nike Shox MR4 mules—to pick up the Grammy for that year’s best rap album.

For further proof of the Biebers’s appreciation for the sportier side of Martine Rose’s offering, look no further than a 2023 Hailey Bieber.

…and here in New York, just a month later.

Rosalía’s appetite for an IYKYK style staple is renowned—as proved by her status as an early adopter of the Nike Shox MR4 in 2022, one of the most hotly coveted products from Rose’s longstanding collaboration with the sports giant.

Proving a Martine Rose track jacket’s irrefutable grail status, Rihanna was repping hers as early as 2022.

Is Pure dating app free? Yes and no.

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

If you've been paying any attention to trends in dating app use lately, you've probably heard about Pure. Last year alone, it nearly doubled its user base, easily reaching the $100 million revenue milestone at a time when many of the best-known dating apps are steadily losing market share.

And if you know that it's the hot new dating app, you're probably also experiencing some FOMO and wondering what it will cost you to give Pure a spin.

The answer is kind of complicated and depends on who you are, but the short answer is that Pure is kind of, sort of free sometimes for some people.

AdultFriendFinder — readers’ pick for casual connections

Tinder — top pick for fast hookups

Hinge — popular choice for regular meetups

Pure — best for anonymous hookups

OkCupid — best app for inclusivity

Pure allows anyone to create an account for free, without handing over their credit card details, but it severely limits what you can actually do in the app as a non-paying member.

Create a profile with photos and fill out their interests/kinks

Browse local ads posted by other users and view their profiles, including their interests/kinks

Browse Pure's extensive database of safety tips and best practices for online dating

Create a dating ad to make your profile discoverable to other users

Send virtual gifts to people who catch your eye

Filter ads based on specific criteria like interests/kinks

There is one major exception, though, and it applies only to women. On Pure, women can do the following without a paid account:

Create a full profile with photos and interests/kinks

But don't think that, just because women get a few extra perks for free, they're starting with a premium membership! If female users want access to premium features like Incognito mode, the ability to browse ads without making yourself visible to other users, or a filter system to help you manage likes, they need to pay for a Queen membership.

Pure offers tiered pricing depending on your subscription commitment. You should expect to pay:

About $14 per week on a weekly subscription

About $30 per month on a monthly subscription

About $100 per year on a yearly subscription

If you're wondering what the purpose of a free Pure membership is if you can't post an ad or talk to other people, you're not alone. If you're not a woman, you have to pony up some cash just to have the chance to talk to another human being, so why not just do so immediately?

Well, there are two major benefits to trying a free Pure account, as far as we can see. The first is that you can start to familiarize yourself with their unique ad system and the swipe-up, swipe-left, and swipe-right mechanics you use to navigate it, which are totally different from most mainstream dating apps and will probably take some getting used to.

But the next benefit is even more important, though it only pertains to people who live outside of big cities. Pure's numbers might be growing at exponential rates, but it's still much more popular in large, metropolitan cities rather than towns and rural areas, so unless you live in Los Angeles, New York, London, Paris, or Berlin, you shouldn't take for granted that there are a large enough number of Pure users near you to make paying for a membership worthwhile.

That's where the free account comes in handy: you can quickly get a sense of how popular Pure is in your area, and if enough people catch your attention, you can decide to invest.

From this perspective, Pure does a nice job balancing utility with the free membership while still making the paid membership enticing.

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Transfer Correspondent AUTHORITY Former Sky Sports and ESPN FC correspondent who specialises in global recruitment, contract negotiations, and high-velocity transfer breaking news. FOCUS Exclusive reporting, domestic and international transfer markets, and elite-level insights. THE INSIGHT Pete utilises a Global Network of Industry Sources to deliver verified, first-to-market reporting. His “Deep-Lens” analysis connects the dots between boardroom strategy and pitch-level recruitment, providing a level of transfer clarity that goes beyond surface-level rumours.

Daniel Farke still has Jaka Bijol firmly in his plans at Leeds despite his recent struggles for regular game time, sources have told Football Insider.

Bijol has been limited to just three Premier League starts for Leeds so far this season.

The 27-year-old impressed in his debut campaign at the club and was a regular under Farke towards the end of last term.

He has found regular minutes harder to come by in recent months, leading to doubts around his long-term future at Elland Road.

As I revealed on the latest edition of Football Insider’s Transfer Insider podcast, Farke is not planning to be open to offers for Bijol in the upcoming transfer window.

He is still in his thoughts for the rest of the campaign, particularly as the Whites are still deploying a three-centre-back formation.

Bijol performed well in 2025-26, with one goal and three assists in 31 appearances for Leeds.

He has only made six appearances so far this term but could soon find himself back in the side regularly if the form of other players drops off or he grabs a chance to impress in the side.

Speaking on the Transfer Insider podcast, I explained: "I don’t think there’ll be any sort of knee-jerk reactions over Bijol right now. He was outstanding for them last year in his debut season at Elland Road.

"The problem for Bijol this season has been that Leeds have had quite a settled side under Daniel Farke. He is still a big part of the plans at Elland Road. He’s under contract until 2030, and Leeds know what a good defender he is, so I’m sure he’ll get plenty of opportunities and it’ll be up to him then to try and keep his place in the team when he does get that opportunity in the side."

Bijol may feel like he deserves more minutes because he has hardly put a foot wrong when playing in league games so far this season.

The Slovenian international is not necessarily a great passer, but ranks highly among top-flight centre-backs for numerous key metrics like duels won (6.63), aerial duels won (4.53) and defensive contributions (13.6) per 90 minutes.

Bijol is also yet to be dispossessed by an opponent, while he is in the 97th percentile (0.7) for clean sheets and 89th percentile (0.7) for goals conceded while on pitch per 90.

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Rizwan is an experienced Crypto journalist with almost half a decade of experience covering everything related to the growing crypto industry — from price analysis to blockchain disruption. During this period, he’s authored more than 3,000 news articles for Coinpedia News.

Rizwan is an experienced Crypto journalist with almost half a decade of experience covering everything related to the growing crypto industry — from price analysis to blockchain disruption. During this period, he’s authored more than 3,000 news articles for Coinpedia News.

Binance France will permanently close affected customer accounts on December 31, 2026.

The local entity failed to secure authorization under the EU’s MiCA framework by June 30.

Users must withdraw remaining assets before closure to avoid additional fees or automatic asset handling.

Binance France is set to shut down customer accounts on December 31 after failing to secure authorization under the European Union’s MiCA framework. With deposits already blocked and new fees coming later this month, French users have a limited window to withdraw their assets before the local platform closes.

Binance France will permanently close accounts tied to its French legal entity on December 31, 2026, following its failure to obtain authorization under the Markets in Crypto-Assets (MiCA) framework.

The exchange has already suspended fiat and cryptocurrency deposits for affected accounts. The closure follows Binance France missing the June 30 deadline to secure the required MiCA authorization.

SHOCKING: BINANCE IS SHUTTING DOWN ACCOUNTS IN FRANCE!After failing to secure MiCA authorization, Binance France is reportedly closing affected accounts on December 31.➡️ Deposits blocked➡️ 0.025% monthly fee (10 $USDC minimum) from Nov 29➡️ Final account closure: Dec 31 pic.twitter.com/z600M31xpW

The move affects accounts held with the French entity and does not mean Binance is shutting down its entire global platform or every French user’s account.

For users still holding assets, however, the timeline is becoming increasingly important.

Binance France will begin charging management fees from November 29 on accounts that continue to hold assets.

The fee will be 0.025% of the account balance per month, subject to a minimum charge of 10 USDC. The measure appears designed to encourage users to withdraw their assets before the final closure date.

The fees will be deducted directly from available USDC balances. If there is not enough USDC, Binance can use other cryptocurrencies held in the account to cover the charge.

Binance has also outlined scheduled withdrawals, with an initial withdrawal expected within three days of November 29, followed by another withdrawal after December 24.

Users who fail to withdraw their assets before December 31 could see their remaining cryptocurrencies handled under Binance’s Terms of Service.

Depending on the circumstances, the assets may be converted, transferred or otherwise dealt with after the account closure. This makes the December 31 deadline more than just an administrative cutoff for affected customers.

For Binance France users, the immediate priority is clear, withdraw eligible assets before the account closure process begins, rather than risk additional fees or the automatic handling of remaining funds.

Personal Finance (Not Investing) • Re: Dropping Medicare Supplement - risks?

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

OP, It would help to know what state your father lives in since some states work differently when it comes to supplemental Medicare insurance.

Our vet relative says that none of the VA hospitals have a trauma center, but they meet the Obamacare minimum requirements. So you first would need to know where they treat burn, gunshot, disaster, and car accident victims. In our area (S. Calif.), the county pays for trauma care but we don't know if the bills are paid right away or if they first have to see if someone else is responsible for the payment (possibly waiting for a court case).

Does your dad acknowledge that they move into an older age pool each year as well as inflation increases the costs of medical care? Saying he will "figure it out" when needed is very short sighted. I've never heard of someone "figuring it out" in real time when they were in pain, unconscious, or being rolled into surgery.

And, as with all insurance, you can't sign up after you need to file a claim. Most of the Medicare add-ons have designated times when you can enroll. And coverage usually starts at a later date. Right now until December 7, it is open enrollment for coverage that takes effect on January 1.

Man Utd Ready to Stun Arsenal With Move for Marcus Tavernier

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Manchester United have been watching Bournemouth star Marcus Tavernier in action ahead of a potential move, according to TEAMtalk.

The 27-year-old winger has taken his game to another level since making the move to the south coast from Middlesbrough in 2022.

Tavernier was a key part of Andoni Iraola's Cherries side that qualified for Europe for the first time last season, with the Leeds-born ace continuing to play a major role under new boss Marco Rose.

Tavernier was in fact on the scoresheet in Bournemouth's recent 3-0 win over the Red Devils at Old Trafford, taking his total to six goals for the season in all competitions.

The Red Devils have plenty of work to do in the transfer market after such a poor start to the campaign under Michael Carrick and TEAMtalk report that Tavernier is among the players being assessed.

However, they will not have it all their own way if they do indeed launch a move for the left-winger.

According to TEAMtalk, Arsenal are keeping close tabs on the player and have been compiling reports as they look to strengthen the left side of their attack.

Arsenal are also huge admirers of Bournemouth's Eli Junior Kroupi and they have also been watching Alex Scott.

Newcastle United are also another club keeping tabs on Tavernier.

Marcus Tavernier's 2026/27 season in numbers

Tavernier unfortunately appears to go under the radar a little bit at Bournemouth with teammates such as Kroupi, Scott and Rayan grabbing the headlines.

He did, however, deservedly come in for praise from pundit Robbie Mustoe on NBC Sports recently after his excellent start to the season.

"We always do an underappreciated performer because we always like to highlight a player that's actually pretty good but doesn't get many accolades," said the former Middlesbrough player.

"Marcus Tavernier, I seem to do him every single season, but his quality is so great. He was at Middlesbrough a little bit, but he wasn't a star play at Middlesbrough, but he's just one of those that always plays well.

"He's got great energy about him, he's got great quality and delivery, crosses and free kicks.  And again, he's the man for scoring another goal for them. He can play as a 10 and he can play on the outside. He's just a wonderful player."

With six goals to his name this season already and some top performances to go along with his output in the final third, it comes as no surprise to see two of England's biggest clubs taking a look at him ahead of a potential transfer.

Charlton Athletic slammed for 'strange' £1m Luton Town transfer

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

After a positive start to the season, it has been a nightmare period for Charlton Athletic in the Championship.

Charlton won their opening three games of the season against Derby County, West Ham United and Preston North End, but they have slid down the table after a dismal run of form in recent months.

It was another bleak evening for Charlton as they were beaten 1-0 by Bolton Wanderers at The Valley on Tuesday night, with Sam Dalby's 18th-minute goal sealing a crucial three points for the Trotters against their fellow strugglers.

The Addicks are now without a win in 11 league games, with their last victory coming at the end of August, and their fourth consecutive defeat saw them drop to 21st in the Championship table, albeit they are still four points clear of the relegation zone.

In an attempt to get his side back into the game, under-fire Charlton manager Nathan Jones introduced Millenic Alli in the 77th minute of the loss to Bolton, but it was another disappointing outing for the 26-year-old.

After scoring one goal and providing two assists in 21 appearances during his loan spell at Portsmouth in the second half of last season, the Addicks signed Alli on a permanent basis from League One outfit Luton Town this summer for an initial fee of £1 million on a four-year contract.

However, Alli has failed to make an impact in southeast London so far, registering just one assist in 14 games in all competitions this season, with only four of those appearances coming from the start, and he came in for strong criticism on social media after Charlton's midweek defeat.

When asked for his thoughts on Alli, FLW's Charlton Athletic fan pundit Chris Woodward claimed that the deal has been a disaster, blasting the Irishman for his poor work rate and lack of effort, and he warned the Addicks that they may find it difficult to offload him in the future.

"I think disaster is an understatement," Chris told FLW.

"My expectations for Alli when he first came in were already on the floor because I thought it was a dreadful signing beforehand, but, somehow, he's managed to perform even worse than that.

"I thought the £1 million price tag was strange for a player that was poor at a struggling Portsmouth last year and at Luton, where he wasn't exactly loved as well.

"I don't want to let my original opinion of Alli prior to signing him cloud my judgement, but I do think this is one of the worst pieces of business we've done in years.

"Our current squad is poor, but I will always like players who try their hardest and give everything for the badge.

"Alli doesn't do that in the slightest, he's lazy and offers nothing, to say the least.

"The fact he doesn't get much game time on the wing for a club that has one proper winger speaks volumes about his ability and effort.

"The fact we have him for four years makes me want to pull my hair out because I can't see anyone else wanting to take him off us.

"I haven't disliked many players under Nathan Jones, even players not up to the required standards, but Alli could be up there.

Having registered just three goal contributions during his loan spell at Portsmouth last season, and only four for Luton in the Championship the previous campaign, it was always unlikely that Alli was going to be the solution to Charlton's attacking woes.

There are serious question marks about why the Addicks opted to spend a seven-figure fee on Alli rather than investing those funds in a more proven option, but the situation highlights one of the biggest issues facing Jones as he looks to arrest his side's current slump.

Should Charlton Athletic look to sell Millenic Alli in January or does he deserve more of a chance at The Valley? Tell us in the comments below!

Alli is far from the only Charlton attacker to have underperformed this season, with Karlan Grant, Charlie Kelman, Matt Godden and Miles Leaburn managing just four goals between them, and, given the lack of quality at his disposal, there could be more misery to come for Jones.

Derby County need to target winter swoop for 25-year-old after Blackburn Rovers summer interest: Opinion

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Derby County are in the Championship relegation zone and have struggled for points. DCFC will get the chance to bolster their squad when the January transfer window opens up for business.

Dundee United left-back Will Ferry was heavily linked with a move to Blackburn Rovers in the summer. However, nothing materialised on that front in the end and he remained in Scotland beyond the deadline in September.

The Rams should now turn their attention to the defender and try and lure him to Pride Park this winter. Ferry, 25, is out of contract at the end of the season and his current club risk losing him for free in June 2027 if they don’t sell him in January, assuming he doesn’t pen an extension anytime soon.

The Bury-born man has made 91 appearances for Dundee United in all competitions to date, eight of which have come in this campaign, and he has chipped in with five goals and an eye-catching 15 assists from the back. He has a nice left foot and should move back down to England soon for a new challenge in his career.

Derby County currently have Craig Forsyth and Derry Murkin in the treatment room. Therefore, DCFC could do with another option in their position on the left flank of defence.

The Rams have had to play the likes of Ryan Hedges and Ryan Nyambe there over recent times. Ferry would be ideal for them now and could plug a gap for the time being.

The Republic of Ireland international, who has one cap to his name, has proved himself up in Scotland. He likes to put crosses into the box as much as possible and attacking players like Carlton Morris and Patrick Agyemang (when he’s fit) will benefit from his delivery.

Ferry rose up through Southampton’s academy before he was loaned out to Crawley Town as a youngster to get some experience under his belt. He then had two permanent years at Cheltenham Town before Dundee United came calling for his services.

Dundee United will want to receive a fee of some sort for Ferry and not lose him for nothing next year. Derby County should try and test their resolve.

The Rams can’t afford to get their recruitment wrong in January and need the right type of characters. At 25, Ferry’s next move is a big one and he needs to go somewhere he is guaranteed regular minutes.

Up next for Derby County is a clash away against Ferry’s old team Southampton after the international break. The Saints are eyeing promotion to the Premier League under Tonda Eckert.

Sources: Liverpool turn attention to signing Bart Verbruggen

📅 Nov 07, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Transfer Correspondent AUTHORITY Former Sky Sports and ESPN FC correspondent who specialises in global recruitment, contract negotiations, and high-velocity transfer breaking news. FOCUS Exclusive reporting, domestic and international transfer markets, and elite-level insights. THE INSIGHT Pete utilises a Global Network of Industry Sources to deliver verified, first-to-market reporting. His “Deep-Lens” analysis connects the dots between boardroom strategy and pitch-level recruitment, providing a level of transfer clarity that goes beyond surface-level rumours.

Liverpool have set their sights on signing Brighton goalkeeper Bart Verbruggen next summer, sources have told Football Insider.

The 24-year-old has enjoyed an outstanding campaign with Brighton since the season began, and has kept 11 clean sheets in 14 appearances across all competitions so far.

Those performances have attracted interest from a number of Europe's biggest clubs, who are now believed to be keeping a close eye on the Netherlands international.

As I revealed on the latest edition of Football Insider's Transfer Insider podcast, Liverpool are among the strong admirers of Verbruggen and could look to tempt him to Anfield.

This comes amid doubts about Alisson's long-term future in net, with his contract set to expire at the end of the season, and work underway to bring in his replacement.

Verbruggen has long been admired at Anfield, despite the interest from elsewhere, and Liverpool are now considering a move to lure him away from Brighton next year.

Football Insider revealed on 6 November that Liverpool are working to identify potential replacements for Alisson as they prepare for him to leave next summer.

The shot-stopper has made some high-profile mistakes already this season, and there is a growing feeling the time may have come for him to finally move on.

Verbruggen has now emerged as a leading contender should Liverpool be forced to strengthen in goal, and they are keeping a close eye on his performances for Brighton.

Speaking on the Transfer Insider podcast, I explained: "Liverpool, like all the top clubs, will be doing homework on a number of players and they'll have contingency plans in place.

"I'm sure all the top clubs across Europe, including Liverpool, will be keeping tabs on Verbruggen because he's a top quality player and he continues to show that for Brighton.

"He's still only 24, playing regularly in the Premier League, and he's a regular in the Netherlands national team as well.

"So it will be more than just Liverpool who will be keeping tabs on him, but he is certainly somebody they could look at if they are forced to sign a replacement for Alisson."

Liverpool are far from the only club interested in signing Verbruggen, with some of Europe's biggest clubs believed to have been watching his strong performances this season.

In the Premier League, the likes of Man United and Chelsea have been named as long-term admirers, while Bayern Munich are among his suitors on the continent.

Meanwhile, Football Insider revealed on 2 November that Real Madrid are keen on Verbruggen, and there is now a feeling he could be tempted to walk away from Brighton.

Sources say that Verbruggen would struggle to turn down the chance to move to Real Madrid if the chance arose, and that has given optimism he could be lured away.

Regardless of Real Madrid's interest, Liverpool could make the first move for Verbruggen, and they will hope they can beat rivals to his signature.

Personal Finance (Not Investing) • Re: Rule of 55/Separation of Service Qs

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Distributions after separation from service after attaining age 55 are not subject to the 10% additional tax (penalty). This is federal law per IRC Sec 72(t). The relevant question to your employer is: After separation from service after attaining age 55, does the plan allow partial withdrawals without restrictions? Some plans may not allow partial withdrawals prior to a certain age.
Right that is a good question. Since my employer 1) allows employees to retire at age 55 or later, AND 2) does not require money to be withdrawn or rolled into another plan (or an IRA) that leads to the conclusion that, yes, they allow partial withdrawals.

Livery of the Week: JetBlue’s Florida Panthers Design

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Edited By: Vanni Gibertini

Editor’s Note: AirlineGeeks is proud to present our ‘Livery of the Week’ series. Every Friday, a team member will share an airline livery, which can be from the past, present, or even a special scheme. Some airline liveries are works of art. The complexity associated with painting around critical flight components and the added weight requires outside-the-box thinking from designers. The average airliner can cost upwards of $200,000 to repaint, creating a separate aircraft repainting industry as a result.

Have an idea for a livery that we should highlight? Drop us a line.

Since the beginning of its operations in 2000, New York-based airline JetBlue has always considered South Florida as one of its primary markets, with a presence at Fort Lauderdale-Hollywood Airport that has grown during the past 25 years to include up to 25 aircraft based overnight at the airport and over 2,000 people supporting its operations in Broward County.

As a sign of its commitment to the South Florida community, JetBlue has announced a multi-year extension for an expanded partnership with the Florida Panthers, the NHL’s southernmost franchise that captured back-to-back Stanley Cups in 2023-24 and 2024-25. The partnership will see JetBlue present a newly redesigned Champions Club at the Amerant Bank Arena in Sunrise, Florida, home of the Panthers, and to celebrate the announcement, the airline unveiled a special Panthers-themed livery for one of its Airbus A320 aircraft.

The new livery draws its inspiration from the Panthers’ jersey, therefore sporting the iconic Panthers’ red across the entire fuselage, with the outline of a golden panther all across the central part of the fuselage, right next to JetBlue’s logo. The Panthers’ shield logo is on the aircraft tail in navy and gold.

Bold red letters spelling FLORIDA appear on the bottom of the aircraft, where there’s also a leaping cat, the team’s logo. More symbols of the Panthers and the State of Florida appear in other parts of the fuselage: a simplified Florida flag appears near the front of the aircraft together with a palm tree and a hockey stick, which represent the secondary logo for the team.

“South Florida has been part of JetBlue’s story since our very first flight more than two decades ago, and we’re continuing to build on that history in a big way as Broward County’s largest airline,” said Marty St. George, JetBlue’s president, in a press release. “The Panthers know a thing or two about winning in South Florida, and we’re bringing that same energy as we expand our network, elevate the customer experience and deepen our commitment to the community. ‘Go Cats Go’ is a fun way to celebrate our shared passion for South Florida and team up with a franchise that knows how to make some noise.”

The aircraft chosen to display this special livery is an Airbus A320, registered as N784JB, delivered to the airline on Feb. 11, 2011.

The original name of the aircraft was “Blue And Infinity And Beyond,” which has been changed to “Go Cats Go” following the repainting in this new livery.

Looking for a new airplane model? Head over to our friends at the Midwest Model Store for a wide selection of airlines and liveries.

NFL Rumors: ESPN Analyst Weighs In on Derrick Henry’s Ravens Future As Giants Emerge As Potential Trade Suitors for Star RB

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

A grueling 33-10 defeat at the hands of the Jacksonville Jaguars may be all it takes to dismantle the Baltimore Ravens totally.

After Tyler Loop missed a 44-yard field goal as time expired in the 2025 season finale, costing Baltimore a playoff berth, the Ravens came into 2026 with a vengeance. Aside from running back Derrick Henry, the team has been plagued by injuries to star players and overall underperformance.

Now, sitting at 4-5 and bottom of an ultra-competitive AFC North, could general manager Eric DeCosta aim to sell ahead of the Tuesday, November 10 deadline?

DeCosta has finalized a trade for 11-year veteran Jacoby Brissett to help fill the massive void left by Lamar Jackson and Tyler Huntley.

While it will certainly not solve all the club's issues, Brissett's 11-year experience will undoubtedly be better than the performance undrafted rookie Joe Fagnano gave in the aforementioned Thursday Night beatdown.

Regardless of how they play from now on, Baltimore may be slipping too far down in an AFC North division that even the Cleveland Browns are contending for. That said, is it possible DeCosta opts to move even bigger pieces and look toward the future?

BE THE GM OF YOUR FAVORITE TEAM: PFN's Free NFL Mock Draft Simulator

During a segment of ESPN's "Get Up," the crew discusses Henry and his ability to make certain fringe teams instant contenders. Host Mike Greenberg asks NFL insider Peter Schrager the likelihood of the bell-cow backer reuniting with his ex-head coach John Harbaugh in New York [Giants].

"I don't see it," said Schrager. "My sense is this, talking to Baltimore's sources: they have 12 draft picks in the upcoming draft, so draft capital doesn't really do anything right now. They also play the Chargers, Panthers, Texans, Bucs the next four weeks. Those are winnable games. And they just traded for a quarterback; they traded for Cam Jurgens."

Baltimore's publicly traceable 2027 draft inventory does not currently add up to 12 picks, and the four opponents Schrager mentioned are the Ravens' next four games rather than four consecutive weeks because Baltimore has a Week 13 bye.

Schrager added, "They're not already in this selloff, fire sale mode. They are not Tennessee, they are not Indianapolis. That said, if there are calls about some other players they would probably be listening to some. I'm talking about guys who might've put themselves as veterans on the trade market themselves by the way that they've been performing in recent weeks."

After describing Baltimore as potentially listening to offers, Schrager affirms his stance that Henry will remain a Raven.

SET YOUR LINEUPS: PFN's Free Fantasy Start/Sit Optimizer

"I do not see Derrick Henry being a piece of that," said Schrager. "As much as Giants fans want to see Harbaugh and Henry reunited."

Second in the league only to Detroit's Jahmyr Gibbs, Henry continues to prove his worth at age 32 with 187 touches this season. Ranked in the top 11 running backs since 2021 using PFN's NFL RB Impact metric, the 6-foot-3 bruiser finds himself ranked 16th in 2026.

However, most of that is likely not the fault of the five-time Pro-Bowler, as Baltimore's offensive line ranks in the bottom half of the league as a whole.

Using PFN's NFL Offensive Line Impact metric, we see they are among the worst in run blocking, with a 74.2 grade. Even worse, the line commits 2.4 penalties a game, one of the highest in the league.

While Schrager believes Henry will stay, the ultimate fate will be undetermined until Tuesday, November 10, when the NFL trade deadline alarm officially sounds off.

Having won 5 of 6, including another rout, the Jaguars could end up as the AFC's top seed

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

No one should be surprised if the AFC playoffs end up running through Jacksonville.

The Jaguars (6-2) have won five of their last six games, including a 33-10 victory in Baltimore on Thursday night, and face the easiest remaining schedule of anyone in the conference. Equally telling: They have shown the kind of resolve that should make them one of the favorites in the AFC.

"The mental and physical toughness of the group I've been pleased with, winning games I guess you're 'quote unquote' supposed to win in ways," coach Liam Coen said. "We came in and took care of business — and have kind of done that a few times now."

Jacksonville rallied last month in London to beat Philadelphia, which played without running back Saquon Barkley, receiver DeVonta Smith, defensive end Jonathan Greenard and three offensive linemen.

The Jags routed Indianapolis last week after the Colts traded team captain DeForest Buckner and longtime guard Quenton Nelson to start a rebuilding effort.

They thumped the Ravens four days later, taking advantage of Baltimore being without starting quarterback Lamar Jackson and backup Tyler Huntley and having to turn to undrafted rookie Joe Fagnano.

Jacksonville has topped 30 points in three consecutive games and owns the largest point differential in the league. The team's plus-108 margin is 50 points better than anyone else, and the Jaguars have won four games by at least 21 points.

"I think we're all realists enough to where we can look at ourselves in the mirror and say we won the game; that's what we came here to do," quarterback Trevor Lawrence said. "Did some good things, but we do have to improve.

"I think you look at the stretch coming up. … It's going to get real, and I think we need to make some improvements and we will. And that needs to be the emphasis."

Jacksonville gets extra time off before playing at Tennessee on Nov. 15. The Jaguars are on the road again the following week against the New York Giants.

"The second-half challenge is going to be being at our best when our best is truly required in some of these games that we have coming up, being road warriors in a lot of ways, with one game being played in Jacksonville in a 65-day span," Coen said. "We have to continue to be focused and together as a group on the road."

The Jaguars, who entered the week leading the NFL in run defense, held Derrick Henry to 57 yards on 17 carries. It was one of his least-productive outings in 16 career games against Jacksonville.

Jacksonville averaged 2.6 yards a carry against the Ravens, with a good chunk of the team's 77 yards rushing coming from 12-year veteran Ameer Abdullah late in the game. Abdullah finished with 30 yards on seven rushes. Bhayshul Tuten averaged 2.4 yards a carry on 12 attempts.

Trevor Lawrence threw for 242 yards and a touchdown at Baltimore, giving him 38 TD passes and 11 interceptions in his last 18 starts. The Jaguars are 15-3 in those games.

Brian Thomas Jr. may have played his final game with Jacksonville. The third-year receiver wasn't targeted in a game for the first time as a pro and played just 19 snaps against the Ravens. He's been the subject of trade rumors, and the trade deadline is Tuesday.

The Jaguars played without nose tackle DaVon Hamilton (leg) and safety Eric Murray (elbow) for the first time this season. But backups Albert Regis and Rayuan Lane played well in their place.

.760 — Coen's winning percentage through 20 games. It's the highest of any NFL head coach since 1950, ahead of John Madden (.759) and Vince Lombardi (.738).

Jacksonville will look to win its fifth in a row — and eighth in nine meetings — against AFC South rival Tennessee.

ORACLE'S ANNUAL MEETING OF STOCKHOLDERS TO BE HELD NOVEMBER 18, 2026

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

AUSTIN, Texas, Nov. 6, 2026 /PRNewswire/ -- Oracle (NYSE: ORCL) will hold its 2026 Annual Meeting of Stockholders on Wednesday, November 18, 2026, at 9:00 a.m., Central Time. This year's meeting will be conducted virtually via a live audio webcast at www.virtualshareholdermeeting.com/ORCL2026. Stockholders as of the close of business on September 21, 2026, the record date, are entitled to participate in the Annual Meeting. Guests may also view the Annual Meeting but may not vote or ask questions.

KB Financial: 3 Key Takeaways for Long-Term Investors

📅 Nov 06, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Net interest margin tightened in Q3, but management expects loan repricing to restore yield momentum as rate hikes flow through.

On its third-quarter 2026 earnings call, held on Oct. 29, 2026, KB Financial (KB -0.58%) announced cumulative Q3 net profit of 5.79 trillion South Korean won (KRW), up 13.1% year over year. The South Korean financial services group relies on balanced earnings across banking and non-banking units to preserve profitability in volatile capital markets.

Net interest margin, or NIM, which measures the profit spread between interest earned on loans and interest paid on deposits, contracted two basis points in the third quarter due to elevated funding costs. Executive leadership expects loan asset repricing from recent central bank interest rate hikes to restore yield momentum in coming quarters.

Net fee income, which represents earnings generated from customer service fees across banking, brokerage, and asset management units, maintained a steady baseline despite broader equity market slowdowns. Diversifying non-banking income streams gives KB Financial predictable revenue across fluctuating market conditions.

The board approved a third-quarter cash distribution of KRW 1,167 per share, representing a 25.5% year-over-year increase. Management remains committed to disciplined capital returns while maintaining a strong common equity tier 1 ratio, or CET1, which measures core regulatory capital strength against risk-weighted assets.

Scale expansion and operational cost discipline are driving cost efficiency, and executive leadership isn't hiding its focus on expanding residual profit after expenses. KB Financial maintains competitive strengths through its dominant retail deposit network, extensive sales channels, and low-cost funding base. Management is prioritizing asset-liability structure alignment and risk-weighted assets management, known as RWA management, to absorb market volatility.

Disciplined risk management and steady asset growth support an ongoing expansion in shareholder distributions. Management targets a mid-to-long-term return on equity, or ROE, near 13% while protecting core profit across rate cycles.

Captive distribution and a growing agent force give CNO Financial a competitive moat that's difficult to replicate in the middle-market insurance space.

On its third-quarter 2026 earnings call, held on Oct. 30, 2026, CNO Financial (CNO -1.92%) announced a 47% increase in operating earnings per diluted share to $1.90. Long-term investors benefit when an insurer converts sales momentum into expanding profit margins. The company's middle-market focus provides a steady foundation for growth. Three key takeaways from management's comments highlight where the business is heading.

Disruption in Medicare Advantage plans is driving older Americans back toward traditional Medicare Supplement coverage. CNO Financial expanded its agent team to capitalize on this migration and serve customers who need personalized advice.

CNO Financial completed its third reinsurance agreement with its Bermuda affiliate, transferring $270 million in statutory life insurance reserves. Moving these liabilities creates regulatory capital efficiency and gives management more flexibility to return cash to shareholders.

Rather than relying on third-party brokers, CNO Financial reaches middle-market clients through its exclusive field agents. This last-mile distribution network protects pricing power and produces recurring insurance sales across both consumer and workplace divisions.

Demand for essential retirement coverage remains steady despite broader inflationary pressures. The insurer relies on a growing agent force, captive distribution, and an exclusive focus on middle-income households to protect its market position while management monitors operating leverage and pricing discipline.

With 17 consecutive quarters of sales growth, the business enters the final quarter of the year with strong operational momentum.

Bradley Cooper is starring in the project as Duke.

Bradley Cooper is starring in the project as Duke, with the film described as a reimagining of the franchise. Joe has been popular for decades as a toy brand and thanks to 1980s cartoons, but never found its footing on the big screen. The hope is this version from McBride, known for his singular voice on shows like East Bound and Down and Righteous Gemstones as well as the recent Halloween movies, can do the trick.
